Third-party notices for Vimkunja
================================

Inventory checked 2026-10-05 against go.mod and the dependency closure of both
entrypoints for Linux, Windows and macOS amd64 (CGO_ENABLED=0).
Include this file and LICENSE with binary distributions. Update this inventory
when dependencies or the Go toolchain change. Each component retains its own
license; Vimkunja's MIT license does not replace these terms.

Excluded: dependency testdata/build tools not compiled into these entrypoints
and the unused modernc.org/memory logo. SQLite itself is public domain; its
modernc Go bindings and runtime dependencies have the notices below.
Source: https://www.sqlite.org/copyright.html
